# Skonester庄园预设：西式 estate / 东亚 east_asian_estate / 日式 japanese_manor。
# 作用域 = 目标角色的 domicile。
# 流程：主宅满级 → 清空外槽 → 6 条外建 staged 满级 → 收尾（可解锁额外外槽）→ 填剩余槽 → 外建链扫满级 → 再收尾内建。

skonester_domicile_force_add_grain_field_max_effect = {
  # domicile_farmland / grain_field
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_01 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_01 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_02 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_02 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_03 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_03 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_04 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_04 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_05 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_05 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_06 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_06 }
}

skonester_domicile_force_add_grain_field_staged_effect = {
  # domicile_farmland / grain_field — staged external + internals
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_01 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_01 }
  hidden_effect = { while = { count = 12 add_random_internal_estate_building = yes } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_02 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_02 }
  hidden_effect = { while = { count = 12 add_random_internal_estate_building = yes } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_03 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_03 }
  hidden_effect = { while = { count = 12 add_random_internal_estate_building = yes } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_04 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_04 }
  hidden_effect = { while = { count = 12 add_random_internal_estate_building = yes } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_05 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_05 }
  hidden_effect = { while = { count = 12 add_random_internal_estate_building = yes } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = grain_field_06 } } add_domicile_building = grain_field_06 }
  hidden_effect = { while = { count = 12 add_random_internal_estate_building = yes } }
}

# 住宅建筑 + 主宅内部升级拉满（主宅逐级升级，避免槽位不解锁）

    trigger = is_domicile_type
    estate = {
      if = { limit = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = estate_main_01 } } add_domicile_building = estate_main_01 }

      skonester_domicile_force_main_villa_internal_tracks_max_effect = yes

# 预设固定 6 条外槽链后，用原版随机外建填满剩余空槽（主宅升级可能解锁额外槽位）
skonester_domicile_fill_remaining_external_slots_effect = {
  switch = {
    trigger = is_domicile_type
    estate = {
      hidden_effect = {
        while = {
          count = 40
          limit = { free_external_domicile_building_slots >= 1 }
          add_random_external_estate_building = yes
        }
      }
    }
    east_asian_estate = {
      hidden_effect = {
        while = {
          count = 40
          limit = { free_external_domicile_building_slots >= 1 }
          add_random_external_east_asian_estate_building = yes
        }
      }
    }
    japanese_manor = {
      hidden_effect = {
        while = {
          count = 40
          limit = { free_external_domicile_building_slots >= 1 }
          add_random_external_japanese_manor_building = yes
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

  skonester_domicile_finish_internals_after_preset_effect = yes

skonester_domicile_preset_apply_on_domicile_switch_effect = {
  switch = {
    trigger = is_domicile_type
    estate = {
      skonester_domicile_estate_preset_full_effect = { PRESET = $PRESET$ }
    }
    east_asian_estate = {
      skonester_domicile_east_asian_estate_preset_full_effect = { PRESET = $PRESET$ }
    }
    japanese_manor = {
      skonester_domicile_japanese_manor_preset_full_effect = { PRESET = $PRESET$ }
    }
  }
}

skonester_domicile_clear_external_apply_on_domicile_switch_effect = {
  switch = {
    trigger = is_domicile_type
    estate = {
      skonester_domicile_clear_external_estate_buildings_effect = yes
    }
    east_asian_estate = {
      skonester_domicile_clear_external_east_asian_estate_buildings_effect = yes
    }
    japanese_manor = {
      skonester_domicile_clear_external_japanese_manor_buildings_effect = yes
    }
  }
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_apply_preset_bonus_effect = {
  remove_character_modifier = skonester_domicile_preset_economy_bonus_modifier
  remove_character_modifier = skonester_domicile_preset_military_bonus_modifier
  remove_character_modifier = skonester_domicile_preset_balanced_bonus_modifier
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_economy_on_character_effect = {
  if = {
    limit = { is_alive = yes exists = domicile }
    domicile = {
      skonester_domicile_cheat_prereqs_effect = yes
      skonester_domicile_preset_apply_on_domicile_switch_effect = { PRESET = economy }
    }
    skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_apply_preset_bonus_effect = yes
    add_character_modifier = skonester_domicile_preset_economy_bonus_modifier
  }
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_military_on_character_effect = {
  if = {
    limit = { is_alive = yes exists = domicile }
    domicile = {
      skonester_domicile_cheat_prereqs_effect = yes
      skonester_domicile_preset_apply_on_domicile_switch_effect = { PRESET = military }
    }
    skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_apply_preset_bonus_effect = yes
    add_character_modifier = skonester_domicile_preset_military_bonus_modifier
  }
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_balanced_on_character_effect = {
  if = {
    limit = { is_alive = yes exists = domicile }
    domicile = {
      skonester_domicile_cheat_prereqs_effect = yes
      skonester_domicile_preset_apply_on_domicile_switch_effect = { PRESET = balanced }
    }
    skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_apply_preset_bonus_effect = yes
    add_character_modifier = skonester_domicile_preset_balanced_bonus_modifier
  }
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_clear_external_on_character_effect = {
  if = {
    limit = { is_alive = yes exists = domicile }
    domicile = {
      skonester_domicile_cheat_prereqs_effect = yes
      skonester_domicile_clear_external_apply_on_domicile_switch_effect = yes
    }
  }
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_economy_apply_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target }
    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  else_if = {
    limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient }
    global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_domicile_holder }
    scope:skonester_domicile_holder = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_economy_on_character_effect = yes
    }
  }
}
skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_military_apply_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target }
    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  else_if = {
    limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient }
    global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_domicile_holder }
    scope:skonester_domicile_holder = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_military_on_character_effect = yes
    }
  }
}
skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_balanced_apply_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target }
    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  else_if = {
    limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient }
    global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_domicile_holder }
    scope:skonester_domicile_holder = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_balanced_on_character_effect = yes
    }
  }
}
skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_clear_external_apply_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target }
    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  else_if = {
    limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient }
    global_var:skonester_cheat_menu_recipient = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_resolve_domicile_holder_effect = yes
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = { exists = scope:skonester_domicile_holder }
    scope:skonester_domicile_holder = {
      skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_clear_external_on_character_effect = yes
    }
  }
}

skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_economy_on_cheat_target_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_economy_apply_effect = yes
}
skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_military_on_cheat_target_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_military_apply_effect = yes
}

  skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_preset_balanced_apply_effect = yes
}
skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_clear_external_on_cheat_target_effect = {
  skonester_cheat_menu_domicile_clear_external_apply_effect = yes
}
